Poker decision update (2026-09-27): the owner does not support Poker and permits
no CP/RVP wagers; silver/gold or no currency were mentioned as possible limits.
No wagered edition is authorized by that ambiguous preference. Keep Poker and
its four rewards locked. A possible free-entry, tournament-only-chip edition
requires agreement on the game itself before implementation. Do not substitute
silver wagering merely because the currency is earned in-game.

checks. The highest-bracket champion receives seven-day status and wings from
one committed result. Native bracket/wager UI and Top-8 currency/item payouts
are outside this earning-route milestone; no unconfirmed achievement is granted.
Deployment and genuine-client visuals remain pending.

M4.1 evidence: [Demon Exterminators rules and validation](DEMON_EXTERMINATORS.md).
The complete 5,100-kill route passed 44 quest checks plus 280 event/progression/
wardrobe regressions. Its final exchange creates five bound EXP Balls and records
11001 atomically. The fixed hunt sequence and item delivery are documented Revora
choices. Native quest-tracker integration, live validation and deployment remain
pending.

M4.2 evidence: [Moon Box rules and validation](MOON_BOX.md). The complete route
passed 31 quest checks and 324 regressions. Six tracked tokens become a Soul Jade;
the selected Life ghost atomically delivers the chosen reward. Only the Moon Box
choice records 11022, as confirmed by the installed achievement condition.
The Meteor ending, refusal and surrender do not award that achievement.

M4.3 evidence: [Power Arena rules and validation](POWER_ARENA.md). The seven-floor
route, saved defeats and 48 badge-pack purchase/opening combinations passed
95 checks and 355 regressions. Clearing floor seven records 11013; twelve badges
earned from Agates record 11014. Counts, rewards and limits are documented Revora
choices. Completion bridges, native tracker and live validation remain open.

M4 bridge prerequisite: corrected the existing quest-list packet allocation and
bounded inbound/outbound records. Nine isolated checks cover 0â€“80 record pages,
pagination beyond 80, malformed requests, unknown IDs and client-authored status.
This supplies no new achievement producer or claim of native-client verification.

M4.4a evidence: [Release the Souls](RELEASE_THE_SOULS.md). Three distinct saved
inspections authorize the guardian's atomic payout and 11012. Native mission
6049 is reconstructed from the saved run. Paid assistance does not grant the
achievement. Thirty-five quest checks and 459 regressions pass; native tracker, animation, reward
text and live display acceptance remain pending. Other Daily Envoy names have
no confirmed matching achievement and are not assigned unrelated IDs.

M4.4b evidence: [Local Arena participation](LOCAL_ARENA_PARTICIPATION.md). Both
first-entry receipts and 11032 commit together after successful match entry.
Twenty-three Arena checks plus 232 progression, wardrobe and Elite PK regressions
pass. Queue requests, spectators and failed transfers do not count. Live acceptance
and deployment remain pending.

M4.4c evidence: [Quiz Show](QUIZ_SHOW.md). The Revora NPC edition provides
weekend sessions, twenty timed questions, sealed answers, competitive results and
atomic bound EXP Ball claims. Thirty-three quiz checks plus 517 regressions pass.
Five correct answers earn 10120; a competitive first place earns 11128.
The unverified native 2068 gameplay family remains disabled globally. NPC live
acceptance and deployment remain pending.

M4.4d evidence: [Weekly and Monthly PK War](LOCAL_PK_WAR.md). General Bravery
admits ordinary players into separate dated level divisions. Survivors cross
three progressively smaller arenas; genuine competitive fields earn 11116/11117,
and eligible sole survivors earn 11118/11119 with a saved purse and dated native
honor. Fifty-nine PK checks and 232 focused regressions pass after the client
halo-identity correction; the preceding batch passed 713 regressions. Checks cover failed transfers,
delayed finalization, concurrent claims, expiry and restart recovery.
BP PK server routes are covered by M4.4g; its native halo and live acceptance remain open.

M4.4e evidence: [Class PK War](CLASS_PK_WAR.md). Envoy 705 admits players by class
and level into private copies of the installed arena. All ten supported families
have separate battles, including Water, Fire and Wind Walker. Genuine winners
earn 11131, a saved atomic purse and a seven-day class halo. The old nobility-based
purse handlers are intercepted. The build, 116 Class PK checks and 386 focused
regressions pass. Native visual/combat acceptance and deployment remain pending.

M4.4f evidence: [Couple PK NPC edition](COUPLE_PK_WAR.md). The guide requires two
ordinary spouses' explicit confirmation, reciprocal saved marriage and a shared
two-person team. Competitive fields earn 11115; both champions earn 11121 and
separate saved purses with seven-day native spouse halos. Defeated spouses can
share their surviving partner's victory. Native paired-animation entry remains
a separate unverified seam. Validation counts and executable hash are recorded
in the contract; genuine-client acceptance and deployment remain pending.

The [classic dungeon checkpoint](CLASSIC_DUNGEON_RESEARCH.md) resolves the
historical Ancestor Box exchange and maps Sky Pass's genuine passes, gates and
monster families. It records why Dis City's four achievement triggers and island
traversal required proof. [Snake Islands](SNAKE_ISLANDS.md) now passes 25 isolated
checks, including its 35-jump crossing and recovery after death.
[Labyrinth](LABYRINTH.md) passes 42 route/payment/loot checks.
[Sky Pass](SKY_PASS.md) passes 34 isolated checks;
its native tracker, map presentation and live client acceptance remain pending.
The newer [Dis City evidence](DIS_CITY_RESTORATION.md) resolves the four stage
associations from previously omitted icon identifiers and records the mapdoc
alias trap. The four combat-stage earning producers now have 75 direct checks;
the difficulty variants and native client acceptance are still pending. The
treasury, Star Sword dresses, Dark Horn direct EXP exchange and next-day rank
prizes now have server implementations and matching private client content for
the current Revora reward edition.

and the recruit/equipment prerequisite gaps. Five audit checks pass. This is a
documented blocker inventory, not completion of the 229 missing achievement paths.
The [growth checkpoint](CHARACTER_GROWTH_ACHIEVEMENTS.md) adds three confirmed
thresholds and fixes two BP contribution defects. Its build and 521 focused checks
pass. The subsequent [BP sharing milestone](BATTLE_POWER_SHARING.md) restores its three threshold producers; native acceptance remains pending.
The [weapon mastery checkpoint](WEAPON_MASTERY_ACHIEVEMENTS.md) adds 33
catalog-mapped proficiency producers, with 75 mastery and 418 regression checks.
The later Scythe checkpoint resolves its installed proficiency identity as 511.
Acquisition of every historical weapon remains unverified; source coverage does
not certify a 320 route.
The [subclass checkpoint](SUBCLASS_PROGRESSION.md) restores seven paid paths and
fourteen new terminal receipts. The 64 direct service checks pass; legacy P1/P9
migration, the broader study wallet and native UI acceptance remain open.

The [rune loadout checkpoint](RUNES.md) adds two equipment receipts and closes
position/swap transaction gaps. Its 36 direct checks pass; native UI acceptance
and deployment remain pending. Dis City reward application prerequisites are
recorded separately in its restoration document.
